生産者物価も大幅上昇し、インフレへの警戒強まる

日本：7月以降の設備投資需要はAI関連産業を中心に旺盛
3
 7～9月期の機械受注は増勢回復へ。AI関連業種からの受注がけん引
― ①旺盛なAI需要のもと、関連業種では設備不足感が強まっていたこと、②輸出増が持続するもとで、好業績が期待され
ること、③中東情勢の不確実性が後退したことなどが背景
 先行き設備投資は緩やかな増勢を辿る見込み
― 半導体製造装置をはじめ、AI・データセンター関連品目を中心に受注残が増加しており、目先こうした傾向が続く見通し
― 積み上がった受注残を段階的に消化することで、設備投資が持続的に下支えされる公算が大きい

世界：AI関連投資の拡大が鉱物需要を押し上げる一方、鈍い資源開発投資の回復ペース
4
 AI関連需要の拡大が銅やレアアースの需給環境に影響
― データセンター建設や電力インフラ整備に伴い、銅やレアアースの需要が拡大。ガリウム等の戦略鉱物への需要も増加
 資源開発投資の回復が遅れるなか、中長期的な供給不足への懸念強まる
―IEAは、2035年に銅が供給量対比で約26％の供給不足に陥ると予測。レアアースは供給不足を回避する見込みながら、

採掘・精錬において中国が支配的地位を占めており、供給元の集中がリスク
IEA：銅、レアアースの需要見通し IEA：銅・レアアースの需給バランス予測（2035年）
（出所）IEA "Global Critical Minerals Outlook 2026"より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
AI関連投資の拡大を背景に、
銅やレアアースの需要が拡大
銅は供給が不足へ
レアアースは供給超過も供給元の集中がリスク
注目点①AI投資拡大と資源供給制約

世界：AI投資が金融市場を下支えする一方、持続性への懸念は残存
 IMFはAI投資や企業収益の拡大を背景に金融市場が底堅さを維持していると評価
― ただし、金融安定リスクは引き続き高いとの評価で、AI、国債市場、サイバーを主要なリスク要因として指摘
 AIについてはAI主導の市場調整リスクを警戒
―AI中核企業の収益拡大によって株価の割高感は緩和する一方、社債発行や循環的な資金調達が拡大。投資収益が期
待を下回った場合、金融市場への影響が増幅される可能性
5

世界：国債市場に残る脆弱性。インフレ・財政懸念による金利急騰リスクは増大
 主要国の長期金利は、期待短期金利とタームプレミアムの双方から上昇
―IMFは財政懸念などを背景にタームプレミアムが歴史的に高い領域にあると指摘
― 国債発行の増加や借換リスクの高まりに加え、価格感応度の高い投資家の存在がインフレ・財政ショックによる金利上
昇を増幅する可能性
― 国債市場は足元で秩序を維持するも、急激な金利上昇は資金調達市場の逼迫や銀行部門への波及を招くリスク

### Page 8

世界：ヘッジファンドのレバレッジ拡大が市場ショックを増幅、銀行部門への波及にも警戒
 高まるヘッジファンドの存在感と市場への影響力
― ヘッジファンドの総資産は2013年の約4兆ドルから2026年初には約13兆ドルに拡大。平時には市場の流動性や効率性
を支える一方、ストレス時にはレバレッジ解消や取引集中が価格変動を増幅させる懸念
 ヘッジファンドの損失がプライムブローカーを通じて銀行部門に波及するリスク
―IMFはヘッジファンドの運用成績悪化がプライムブローカーの収益にも悪影響を及ぼすと分析。銀行自身の健全性に加
え、ノンバンクとの相互連関を通じた金融システム全体への波及にも警鐘
7

世界：2026年の世界経済は底堅く推移も、経済成長の恩恵やインフレの負担は一部に偏在
 IMFは2026年の世界成長率を小幅に上方修正（3.0％→3.1％）
―AIブームを追い風とした半導体輸出の増加等を背景に、アジアを中心に上方修正
― 備蓄放出・代替調達等による政府・企業の対応が原油供給ショックによる下押しを緩和したことも影響
 一方、AI需要の取り込みや供給ショックへの耐性には国家間で格差
―IMFによれば、世界の経済成長の95％を占める国の数は54（4月）から32（10月）に減少したほか、今次改定では47％
の国が成長率を下方修正。AIブームの恩恵や供給ショックによる負担は一部の国・地域に偏在

（年／月）
日本：コメ価格下落で企業物価は鈍化。もっとも、先行きは原油高を背景に再加速へ
18
 9月の国内企業物価はコメ価格の下落を主因に、前年比＋6.8％（前月：同＋7.6％）と鈍化
― 新米価格が昨年を下回ったこと等を背景に、コメ価格が大幅に下落。国内企業物価を▲0.75%Pt押し下げ
― 一方、3月来の原油高を背景に化学・プラスチック製品は高止まり。また、AI需要等を背景に非鉄金属も高い伸びを維持
 7月以降の原油高再燃を背景に、先行きの国内企業物価は再加速する見込み
― 原油価格高止まりにタンカー運賃の急騰が重なり、先行きの輸入物価は原油関連を中心に加速へ
― 国内企業物価は石油・石炭製品やラグを伴いつつ化学・プラスチック製品などを中心に再加速する見込み
（注） コメは玄米及び精米。食品関連は、飲食料品及び農林水産物（除くコメ）

（出所） 日本銀行「企業物価指数」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
国内企業物価 輸入物価（円建て）と国内企業物価
（注） 輸入物価は1カ月先行。原油輸入単価とドバイ原油（1カ月先行）を使用し、輸入物価（契
約通貨建て）の先行きを試算し、為替は1ドル157円程度の推移を想定。二重線部分は見
通し
（出所） 日本銀行「企業物価指数」、財務省「貿易統計」、IMF、LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國聯準會如市場預期 ，維持政策利率不變。 決策官員仍對通膨保持擔憂 ， 也承
認美國經濟正以「強勁速度」成長。聯準會主席Kevin Warsh在會後談話中，並
未明確排除進一步緊縮政策的可能，激勵市場對12 月升息的預期升溫。數據方
面，美國第三季 GDP 年化成長率達 3.4%，高於前值的 2.2%，亦優於市場預期
的 3.2%，印證美國經濟動能強勁。此外，9 月 PCE 與核心 PCE 符合預期，但凸
顯通膨壓力持續偏高。在 「通膨高企」 與 「經濟強韌」 雙重因素驅動下，市場預
期聯準會將於 12 月升息，為美元指數提供有力支撐。惟近期受 AI 產業股票波
動加劇影響，部分資金轉入美債避險，導致美元指數在高檔面臨調節性賣壓。
⚫ 儘管受能源價格走揚影響，9 月歐元區通膨率升至 3.8%，但在薪資壓力維持溫
和，且歐洲央行尚未觀察到第二輪通膨效應全面擴散的情況下 ，歐洲央行上週決
議維持政策利率不變 。通膨回升令市場預期歐洲央行將在下次會議升息 ， 為歐元

提供部分支撐 ， 但考量歐元區經濟成長表現仍落後美國 ， 在美元基本面強勁的抑
制下 ， 歐元反彈力道相對有限 。 目前歐元最大的潛在風險在於部分國家的債券市
場壓力尚未消散，市場對歐洲財政風險仍保持高度警戒。
⚫ 日本央行維持政策利率於 1.25%不變，並且首次明確表示已達成其 2%的通膨目
標；過去日本央行雖認為通膨改善 ， 但始終強調尚未穩定達標，如今首次正式承
認達成 2%目標，代表未來升息門檻進一步下降。此外，日本央行將經濟成長預
測維持在 0.7%至 0.8%，並小幅上修 CPI 預測，令市場預期日央 12 月有望再次
升息，日本貨幣政策正常化將有助日圓結束疲軟走勢。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.390，低點在 32.676，以 32.478 作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 3 分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，市場等待聯準會利率決議，交投偏謹慎觀望，而外
資在前周大幅匯出之後，轉為雙向進出，台幣止跌回穩，最高來到 32.390 的周內高
點；然而周間，美國科技股財報表現失色，台股賣壓沉重，外資賣超台股並再度轉
為大幅匯出，台幣走貶，最低來到 32.676，不過匯價逢高吸引出口商賣壓出籠，加
上央行提供流動性，台幣貶幅收斂，終場在央行調節之下收在 32.478。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣偏弱震盪。就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持利率不
變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，且聯準會表示通膨仍處於高檔，顯示聯準會對通
膨仍保持警戒，市場預期美國利率將維持高檔，台美利差仍維持在相對高位，不利
台幣表現；另一方面，美國科技股財報表現不如預期，引發市場對AI 投資回報的疑
慮，對台股也帶來壓力。就台灣市場來看，外資持續調節台股並大幅匯出，10 月累
計賣超將近兆元，資金匯出壓力大，不過一方面央行持續進場調節，加上匯價逢高
預計吸引出口商進場拋匯，料將限制台幣貶值速度。綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢
偏弱。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.300-32.800

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣持續走升。周初市場等待美國聯準會利率決議，交投偏向觀望，境
外人民幣主要於 6.6600 上方震盪； 周間聯準會如預期維持利率不變 ，美元指數自 102
上方回落 ， 加上中國出口結匯需求持續提供支撐， 境外人民幣突破6.6600 整數關口；
週五人行中間價續創近三年九個月新高，最高來到 6.7138，顯示中國人行允許人民
幣溫和升值的走勢，境外人民幣一度升破 6.6500 整數關口，最高來到 6.6487，終場
收在 6.6528，週線升值 162 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6558，週線升值 146 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升 。 就中國國內市場來看 ，10 月官方製造業 PMI 降至 49.3，
重新落入景氣收縮區間，反映中國內需及製造業動能仍有待改善，不過 9 月出口年
增 36.3%，出口商結匯需求持續提供人民幣支撐，加上人行中間價續創近三年九個
月新高，預計將持續帶動人民幣走升；就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持
利率不變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，顯示聯準會對通膨仍保持警戒，市場預期
美國利率將維持高檔 ， 美中利差維持在相對高位 ，將限制人民幣升值的速度與幅度 ；
最後就技術面來看 ，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散 ，匯價維持在五日均
線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣
偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6200-6.6700

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Nov/02/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 上漲。週初，美伊衝突仍陷僵局支撐美元避險買盤，USD/JPY 最
高來到 158.34；週間，聯準會公布利率維持利率不變，USD/JPY 持續在 158 附近盤
整；臨近週末，日本央行公布利率維持不變並釋放偏鷹訊號， USD/JPY 最低來到
156.94，終場收在 157.53，週線上漲 72 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本央行於上周五宣布利
率維持在 1.25%不變，並表示隨著基礎通膨率大致達到 2%，央行必須持續控制通
膨穩定於此目標，且再次強調貨幣政策已進入更為緊縮階段，未來每隔數月即可能
升息。此外，日本央行在季度報告中，亦將截至 2028 年 3 月財年的核心消費者通
膨預估自三個月前預測的 2.4%上調至 2.6%。日本央行偏鷹立場令市場預期日本最
快將於 12 月再次升息，預計將有利日圓表現。二、聯準會宣布維持利率於 3.75%-
4%區間不變，惟因通膨仍處高位，聯準會重申將致力實現 2%的長期通膨區間， 
FedWatch 顯示 12 月升息機率約七成，美元持穩，預計將限縮 USD/JPY 下行空
間。三、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY 各天期均線趨平，近期匯價大致
在 156 至 158 間震盪，匯價暫無明確方向性。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現
區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日圓：156.00-158.50

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Nov/02/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，美伊衝突持續以及法國政經情勢動盪等情況下，
歐元走跌，最低來到 1.1111；週間，聯準會和歐洲央行相繼公布利率決議均維持不
變，歐洲央行發表言論偏鷹派，歐元止跌回升至 1.1214；臨近週末，歐元區公布經
濟數據好壞參半，歐元自高點回落，終場收在 1.1158，週線下跌 41 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行如市場預期宣布
存款利率維持在 2.5%不變，並警告能源成本持續上漲將對通膨造成上行風險，暗示
可能在 12 月再次升息 ， 升息預期升溫預計將支撐歐元走勢 。二 、 歐元區第三季GDP
年增 1.3%， 高於市場預期的1.1%， 顯示在能源價格持續上漲的不利因素下 ，經濟仍
展現韌性，預計有利歐元表現。三、聯準會宣布維持利率在 3.75%-4%不變，並重申
致力控制通膨的立場 。 此外 ，美國公布第三季 GDP 成長 3.4%， 優於市場預期的3.2%，
9 月核心 PCE 年增率自前月的 3%上升至 3.1%，在美國經濟強勁及通膨仍高的情況
下 ， 市場預期聯準會將於 12 月升息一碼，美元持穩 ，預計將限縮歐元走升空間 。四、
技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 各天期均線向下發散，不過近期匯價在多次向
下測試 1.1120 附近均未有效跌破 ， 顯示下方仍有買盤支撐。綜合以上 ， 預估EUR/USD
本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

增發定存單，令市場資金充裕。近兩日股市回落，令週五資金需求上揚，惟供
需尚屬平衡，因此短率持穩於近期區間。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.825% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在

5 年期指標券 A15108 議價成交在 1.845%~1.846%。

膨容忍度變寬之態度 ， 惟市場對央行升息預期並未完全消除 ， 買盤追價動力有
限，預期台債殖利率維持區間整理，關注下週30 年期公債標售果，預估 5 年
券區間為 1.80%~1.85%，10 年券區間為 1.99%~2.04%。

營 運 分 析

聯發科為全球前五大 IC設計公司 
聯發科是台灣最大 IC設計公司，也是全球前五大無廠半導體(fabless)公司，
成立於 1997/05，並於 2001/07 掛牌上市。在無線通訊與數位媒體領域布局
多年。26Q3 三大產品線分為 Mobile Phone 37%、Smart Edge Platform 
58%、Power IC 5%，Mobile Phone產品以手機應用程式處理器(Application 
Processor)為主；Smart Edge Platform產品包括路由器、寬頻網路、藍芽耳
機等各種有線無線晶片、ARM架構運算單晶片、智慧電視、數位機上盒，以
及客製化晶片服務。

中心 ASIC 的毛利率略低於公司平均，但因為供應鏈成本上升，漲價反應，
且資料中心業務的營業費用率低，營益率高於公司平均，26Q4 起加分效益
顯現。

非中國客戶。

SerDes已 tape out。 
26Q3 完成 39 億美元海外 CB 發行，NVIDIA、Alphabet 皆參與認購，有助
於未來 AI生態系合作。

### Page 1

M
Idea
Further upside surprise: In our recent CPO report, we raised WIN Semi's revenue
forecasts  to reflect stronger demand for satellite communications RF, driven by
major customer SpaceX (SPCX; covered by Adam Jonas). However, we
underestimated the favorable pricing and margin, as evidenced by the 28 Oct   3Q26
results. Reflecting the much higher gross margin, we raise our 2026, 2027 and 2028
EPS by 35%, 28% and 17%, respectively, and raise our price target 17% to  NT$700  to

capture the long-term upside from both new businesses - space communications
and AI optical networking laser.
3Q26 margin beat: Core margin reached 40.7% in 3Q26, well above our original
forecast of 29%. The company indicated space communications revenue already
represents 40% of its infrastructure business, accounting for 35-40% of total
revenue. This implies the space-related revenue is tracking at c.15% of total revenue
in 3Q. Despite a relatively  small portion of revenue, we estimate the business could

lift 3Q26 margin by 5% (half of 3Q26 increase), implying the space communications
margin could be >60%, well above our and Street expectations. V3 satellite
development will be a key growth driver into 2027. See Exhibit 1  for our analysis.
4Q26 revenue guidance looks solid: Smartphone and WiFi revenue remain stable
despite weak China smartphone demand: Win Semi now has higher exposure to
Apple iPhone through high-band PA (Broadcom) and WiFi (Murata). As such,

revenue can still grow single digit QoQ in 4Q26, despite recent weakness in China
smartphone demand. Please see Exhibit 3  and Exhibit 4    for  our earnings review.
Upgrade to OW: The stock trades at 25x our new 2028 EPS. Given the significant
revenue contribution from space communications and AI lasers, we think our
implied target multiple of 29x 2028 EPS should be justified. Win Semi historically
traded at 40x PE during the smartphone 3D sensing product cycle.

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Surprising Margin Expansion from Optical/Space Businesses
WIN Semi   could benefit from PD growth and potential LD outsourcing
by late 2027
In our previous WIN Semi report, we argued that Lumentum (LITE.O, covered by Meta
Marshall) was unlikely to outsource CW lasers or EML production to WIN Semi, a key
pillar of our earlier UW rating. However, our recent  checks with WIN Semi management
suggest that WIN Semi and its major customer, we assume it's Broadcom (AVGO, covered

by Joseph Moore), have secured sufficient InP substrate supply for WIN Semi's PD
production, potentially supporting substantial growth for the PD (photo diode) business
in 2H26. We estimate PD could account for >10% of WIN Semi's total revenue. Our recent
checks from the supply chain also suggest that WIN Semi's largest PD customer  could
outsource LD (laser diode) production to WIN Semi.
We also see the potential for even stronger revenue growth from WIN Semi's aerospace

business into 2027, especially from the ground station and satellite business. With
SpaceX's  launch of Starship and Starlink V3 satellite, we expect WIN Semi's content value
per satellite   to increase significantly from Starlink v2 to v3, with potential upside from
future DTD (direct to device) satellites. The increase in content value comes not only from
higher RF chips volumes, but also from the migration from GaAs to GaN. Based on our
industry checks, the ASP for 4-inch GaN could be US$9k, compared with ~US$2.5-3k for 6-

inch GaAs. We therefore upgrade our rating from EW to OW with the strong gross margin
performance, which we believe to be >60%.
Where we could be wrong with our OW call: We think the largest near-term swing factor
for WIN Semi is the aerospace business, which is relatively difficult to track given the
inherent risk of delays or failures in satellite launches. However, on September 28, 2026,
SpaceX successfully launched the Starship with Starlink V3 satellite. If SpaceX decides to

speed up the satellite constellation, there also could be upside to our forecasts.
All of our satellite launch numbers are based on Adam Jonas's SpaceX forecasts. Please
see his latest report: SpaceX: SpaceX/Tesla and SI's Next Act (23 Oct 2026)

3Q26 earnings and 4Q26 guidance review - margin and earnings beat on

strong optical and aerospace demand
WIN Semi delivered a solid 3Q26 performance, with margins and earnings exceeding
expectations, supported by robust demand across its optical and aerospace businesses.
The gross margin beat MSe and consensus by 7-8ppts.  Strength in optical applications,
driven by continued investment in AI-related optical connectivity and high-speed data
transmission, alongside resilient aerospace demand, supported a favorable product mix

and improved manufacturing efficiency.
The higher contribution from these relatively high-value-added segments lifted gross
margins above expectations, driving positive operating leverage and stronger-than-
anticipated earnings. Looking ahead, we expect sustained momentum in optical
communications and aerospace applications to remain key drivers of WIN Semi's
profitability.

WIN Semi: Valuation Methodology
Our price target (base-case value) increases from NT$600 to NT$700: This also factors
in our EPS forecast changes  for 2025 to 2028.
We continue to derive our price target from a residual income model as we believe it best
presents the value of the company. All of our other key assumptions are unchanged,
including a cost of equity of 8.0% (beta of 1.0, equity risk premium of 6% and risk-free

rate of 2%), intermediate growth rate assumption of 15% and terminal growth rate of
4.0%.
Our bull-case value  increases  from NT$900 to NT$1050 and our bear-case value from NT
$300 to NT$350, implying 44x and 14x 2028e EPS, respectively.

MS Rating
▪ Relatively strong iPhone demand (vs.
Android) appears likely in the near term, but
smartphone growth is mature compared to

AI demand.
▪ We expect to see more GaAs wafer
consumption as we migrate to the WiFi 7
era, as WiFi 7 has three bands (2.4 GHz, 5
GHz and 6 GHz) vs. WiFi 6 at just two (2.4
GHz, 5 GHz).
▪ GaN penetration in satellite business could
lift the wafer ASP.
▪ We think optical transceivers and robotics
may become future drivers, as WIN Semi has
the technology to enable products like EML,
CW-DFB and DML.
▪ We are OW given the strong margin
improvement from the new business.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

VPEC (2455.TW): Optoelectronics mix upgrade driving GM expansion; 
3Q26 in line; Buy
2 November 2026 | 5:30AM HKT
3Q26 revenues increased 39% QoQ, with optoelectronics (mainly InP epiwafer for PD 
and CW lasers) revenues contribution up to 43% (vs. 27% in 2Q26), driving GM up to 
45% (vs. 39% in 2Q26). Along with opex ratio further improved to 13.7% (vs. 15.4% 
in 2Q26), VPEC’s OP income increased 87% QoQ in 3Q26, or 2% behind / 7% above 
our / Bloomberg consensus estimates. Looking ahead, we expect 4Q26E revenues to 
further increase by 14% QoQ, with GM up to 46% driven by product mix upgrade 
toward Optoelectronics. The company also announced its issuance of unsecured 
convertible bonds valued at NT$1.5bn (US$46m), to supplement its procurement in 
raw materials and equipment, reflecting the strong demand and VPEC’s commitment 
in capacity expansion; as we highlighted in Oct (Global Server TAM updates) where 
our US Internet team forecast that leading US cloud service providers (CSPs) in 
aggregate would grow their capex budgets by 84%/ 52%/ 13% YoY in 2026E-28E 
and where we raise our estimates by 4% / 17% / 22% in 2026-28E to US$1,397bn by 
2028E. We remain positive view on VPEC, which we believe is positioned to benefit 
from the AI server racks ramp up, optical networking specification upgrades across 
transmitters / CW lasers (toward 1.6T optical modules) and receivers (toward 200G 
PD), product expansion toward EML base epiwafers, further customer penetration 
for CW lasers, improving InP substrate supply, and opportunities in InP epiwafers for 
high power lasers for NPO and CPO. Maintain Buy.

3-month revenue preview: VPEC delivered solid revenue growth at 39% QoQ / 47% 
YoY in 3Q26; Looking ahead, we expect +14% QoQ in 4Q26E, riding on new AI server 
racks ramp up and optical networking specification upgrade toward 1.6T optical

Earnings revision: We factor in 3Q26 results and cut our net income by 7% in 2026E, to 
reflect modest revenue moderation from smartphone RF pacing alongside a higher R&D 
expense ratio for AI data center products. Despite the cut, we continue to expect 
sequentially higher revenues in 4Q26E with GM continuing to expand in 4Q26E vs. 3Q26, 
driven by new AI server racks ramp up and optical networking specification upgrade 
toward 1.6T optical modules, along with capacity expansion and better InP substrate 
supply to better capture the demand. We keep our 2027E-28E estimates largely 
unchanged.

Valuation: We continue to derive our 12m target price from 2029E P/E discounted back 
to 2027E at 10.5% COE (no change), and derive our target P/E multiple from refreshed 
peers’ trading P/E vs. forward year fundamentals (NI YoY and OPM). Based on our 
2029E-30E NI YoY of 18% (unchanged) and 2029-30E OPM of 38% (unchanged), and 
peers’ PEG&M of 0.4x (unchanged), we derive target P/E multiple at 22.0x (vs. 22.2x 
previously), reflecting a slight peer group multiple compression amid the product 
transition window from 800G to 1.6T optical transceivers, balanced by sustained 
long-term AI optical networking demand. Our new 12-month target price is at NT$785 
(vs. NT$793 previously). Our new TP implies 2027E P/E at 68.7x (vs. 69.4x previously), 
within the company’s trading range (vs. peak at 74x, and +1stv. at 47x). Maintain Buy.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that

Greater China Technology Semiconductors  | Asia

Cloud Semis: Growing Cloud
Capex into 2027
Hyperscalers’ spending remains strong so far, with further
expansion signaled for 2027 amid FCF turning negative. Positive
read for cloud semis (Aspeed and Montage).
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst
Daniel.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2863
Charlie Chan
Equity Analyst

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey. Voting
opens 10 November 2026.
Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
2027 capex growth is highlighted: The Big Four hyperscalers' combined C3Q capex
was US$185bn, +90% Y/Y. CY2026 guidance stands at approximately US$170bn for
Microsoft, US$195–205bn for Google, US$135–145bn for Meta, and US$220bn for
Amazon. Google and Amazon maintained guidance; Meta raised its spending floor

US$5bn. More importantly, Microsoft expects spending to accelerate in January–
June 2027, Google anticipates a significant increase in 2027, and Amazon expects
meaningfully higher spending. Meta also plans to keep investing aggressively.
Supply constraints remain the issue: Microsoft reported C3Q26 capex of US
$45.2bn and expects C4Q spending >US$50bn, with some capacity deliveries
shifting into 1H27. Google invested US$51.4bn and turned to third-party computing

late in C3Q to address supply constraints, with usage rising in C4Q. Meta invested
US$34.9bn, including finance lease principal payments; Amazon’s cash capex
reached US$75.9bn. Inference already contributes well over half of AWS AI revenue.
Debating monetization: Microsoft generated US$18.6bn of quarterly FCF and
expects positive FCF in FY2027. Google, Meta, and Amazon reported negative
quarterly FCF of US$9.1bn, US$2.1bn, and US$33.5bn, respectively. Google also

flagged depreciation, data center operating costs, and third-party computing
expenses as near-term cloud margin headwinds, alongside TPU inventory cash
requirements. Management remains focused on attractive long-term returns.
Cloud semis: Constructive read-through. Sustained server investment supports
Aspeed's and Montage's demand outlook; delayed capacity could shift revenue
between quarters, but it's just a timing issue. Approximately 75% of Microsoft’s C3Q

capex targeted short-lived assets, including CPUs, GPUs, and higher memory/storage
costs, up from two-thirds mentioned before. Google allocated ~65% to servers and
networking.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
2027 capex is highlighted: Microsoft, Google and Amazon flag higher spending;
Meta remains committed to aggressive investment.
Capacity constraints, not budget: Microsoft’s delivery delays and Google’s third-
party computing usage point to infrastructure bottlenecks.
Cloud semis: Constructive read-through so far. Sustained hyperscaler spending

provides a favorable backdrop, with capacity additions raising demand.
November 2, 2026 01:07 AM GMT

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

 상대 (3.3) (21.1) (22.1)  
 절대 (달러환산) (15.2) (10.5) 21.4

3Q26 OP 2.7조, 당사 추정치 35% 상회 
서프라이즈의 근거가 판매보증비 감소에서 발생. 3Q26 기말환율의 낙폭 (-12% qoq)으로 
인한 역설적 비용절감의 규모가 전년동기 대비 1.2조 원을 넘어서며 환율의 조사모삼 효과
로 만들어진 컨센서스 상회 실적. 
긍정적인 부분: 컨틴전시, 신차 효과, 인센티브, 생산 정상화 
지속적인 컨틴전시 체제를 유지하며 비용절감 실천 (=지속된 요소). 4분기 추가적으로 나아
질 요인은 1) 내수 신차효과로 판가 상승 및 유기적 수익성 개선, 2) 공장 가동률 정상화를 
통한 고정비 부담 절감, 3) 해외시장 신차효과를 통한 딜러 인센티브 절감. 
경계할 부분: 재료비, 환율, BEV 판매비중 확대 
재료비 측면에서 1) 2Q26 시작된 미국-이란 전쟁 관련 원재료비 상승은 4Q26F에도 하락
세를 보이지 못할 것이며, 2) 구리, 철강 등의 금속 원자재의 가격 상승 또한 4Q26F 지속될 
것. 또한 3Q26과 반대로 qoq 기말환율 대비 낙폭 확대할 평균환율 (mtd 1,338원) 효과로 
환 관련 감익 본격화, IONIQ 3의 출시는 인센티브 절감인 동시에 믹스 관점에서 부정적. 
투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 440,000원으로 소폭 상향 
목표주가 상향 근거는 컨틴전시 체제 유지를 통한 FY27F 자동차사업 부문 이익 예상치 상
향. 부정적 외생변수 (환율, 재료비)가 지속된다는 전제 하에 FY27F OPM 하락을 예상하나, 
(FY26F 5.6%, FY27F 5.2%) 향후 신차효과와 생산 정상화에 따른 자동차 사업부문 수익성 
반등의 여력 존재. 주가 상승 트리거는 1) CES 전후 BD 관련 기대감, 2) 신차 출시 이후 견
조한 판매량, 3) 환율, 원재료비 등 외생변수의 개선.

주가 추이

3Q 큰 폭의 판매 감소, 매출/영업이익 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영 업 이 익 은 각각 44.6조원(-4.5% 이하 Y oY)과 2.4조원
(-5.4%) 기록하며 시장 기대치 하회 전망. 3Q 매출액은 글로벌 판매 부진 지속
과 환율 하락, 그리고 친환경차 판매 성장 둔화 등의 영향에 기인. 특히, 3Q 판
매량은 918.7만대(-11.5% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동기 대비 큰 폭 감소하며 매출
액에 부정적 영향. 이는 7월과 8월 발생한 파업 영향으로 약 5.5만대 규모의 생
산 물량 차질이 발 생 한 부분에 기인. 파업은 기존 물량/믹스 개선에 기 여 했 던 
글로벌 HEV 판매도 둔화(+3.4%)시키면서 매출에 부정적 영향을 심화. 
3Q 수 익 성 은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입에 따른 원가 상승 영향과 친환경차 
판매 감소에 따른 부정적 믹스 영향 불 가 피. 이에 시장 기대치인 3.0조원을 크
게 하회할 전망. 다만, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮아지면서 판매보증비 
외화환산손익 영향은 다소 개선(0.4%p) 예상되며, 관세 영향 또한 전년 동기 
대비 약 1조원(2.1%p) 감소하면서 수익성 악화 영향을 일부 만 회 할 전망.  
’27년 지연된 신차효과 가시화로 반등 여력 충분 
4Q부터는 3Q 파업 영향 등으로 지연된 신차 확대 본격화되고, 현지 수요 높은 
차종 중심의 물량 확대를 통해 매출 회복 및 이익 성장 가시화가 기대. 아 반 떼
와 투싼 FMC 확대로 국내 및 해외 판매 둔화가 점진적 만회되고, 지난 9월부
터 터키에서 아이오닉3 생산이 본격화되면서 유럽에서의 시장 부진 회복이 기
대. 또한, GV80과 G80 HEV 모델 출시로 내년부터 국내/북미 중심 판매 확대
되며 올해 부진했던 물량/믹스 개선에 기여할 것으로 기대. ’27년 HMGMA에
서 HEV 신차 생산 투입 예정되어 있어 상 대 적 으 로 높은 수요가 지속되는 북미
에서의 HEV 현지 생산 확대로 가동률 증 가 와 관세 부담 완화도 수익성 개선에 
기여할 것으로 기대. 
부정적 환율 고려 필요하나 ’27년 강한 증익 기대  
’27년 동사 영 업 이 익 추 정 치 는 12.4조원(+22.3%)으로 ’26년 감 익 에 서 ’27년 
증익 전환 예상. 최근 환율 추세 고려 시 내년 상 반 기 까 지 부정적인 환율 영향 
확대 가 능 성 존재하나, 기존 노후 차 종 에 서 현지 수요 대응 가능한 신차 중심 
생산/판매 확대로 물량/믹스 개선되며 부 정 적 환율 영향 상쇄 기대.  
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-11.8%)과 상반기 적 용 한 코 스 피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)에 따라 목 표 주 가 를 670,000원으로 하향. 그러나, 연 내 RMAC 운영 
성 과 와 RA 설립, 12 월 모셔널 자 율 주 행 상용화와 웨 이 모向 로 보 택 시 
양산/공급 등 미래 사 업 에 서 의 가 시 성 도 여전히 기대 가능한 부분.

반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

현대차 (005380) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

www.imeritz.com

삼성바이오로직스 (207940)

Company Brief 
제약/바이오 
Analyst 김준영 
junyoung.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 김현비 
hyunbee.kim@meritz.co.kr 외형 성장 지속, 수익성 개선은 시간이 필요

3Q26 Review  
3Q26 연결 기준 매출액 1조 4,903억원(YoY +18.5%, QoQ +12.8%), 영업이익 
6,401억원(YoY +1.0%, QoQ +9.2%)으로 컨센서스를 각각 9.8%, 4.7% 상회했다. 
기존 1 ~ 4공장 풀가동 및 미국 Rockville 공장 매출 약 1,000억원과 5공장 PPQ 
물량 인식이 더해지며 외형 성장을 견인했다. 반면 신규 생산시설의 초기 비용 및 제
품 믹스 영향으로 OPM은 42.9%를 기록하며 전년 동기 대비 7.5%p 하락했다. 동
사는 연간 매출 성장률 가이던스를 기존 +15~20%에서 +20%로 상향했다. 당사는 
4분기 Rockville 공장 매출 기여와 기존 공장의 높은 가동률을 감안하여 연간 매출
액 5조 6,135억원(YoY +23.2%)을 예상한다.

신규 생산거점의 외형 확대, 이익 기여는 좀 더 긴 호흡으로 
5공장은 2Q26부터 PPQ 생산을 본격화했으나 상업 생산에 따른 실질적인 이익 기
여는 2027년 하반기에 확대될 전망이다. Rockville 공장은 3Q와 4Q 각각 약 
1,000억원의 매출이 예상되며 2027년 분기별 매출도 유사한 수준을 유지할 것으로 
전망한다. 다만 영업이익 기여까지는 시간이 더 소요될 것으로 예상되어 2027년 이
익 성장은 미국 공장보다 5공장 Ramp-up이 주도할 전망이다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원 유지 
삼성바이오로직스에 대해 투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 200만원을 유지한다. 고객사 수주 
문의가 회복되는 가운데 대형 계약 협의가 진행 중이며 6공장 역시 연말 또는 내년 
초 착공을 목표로 하고 있다. 다만 신규 수주가 실제 계약으로 이어지는 시점과 5공
장의 상업 생산 전환 속도가 중기 실적의 핵심 변수로 판단한다. 추가 대형 수주 확
보 시 5공장 이후의 성장 가시성도 높아질 것으로 예상한다. 
PolyPeptide 인수는 펩타이드 CDMO로 사업을 확장한다는 점에서 긍정적이다. 인
수 완료 이후 다음 분기부터 연결 매출 인식이 가능하나 유의미한 영업이익 기여까지
는 최소 2년 이상 소요될 전망이다. PolyPeptide 인수는 현재 추정치에서 반영하지 
않았으며 향후 인수 완료 시 연결 OPM 희석 가능성을 감안할 필요가 있다. 한편 최
근 법원 항고심에서 배양부터 정제까지의 생산공정이 쟁의행위 기간에도 정상 수행되
어야 할 보전작업으로 인정되면서 파업에 따른 생산 차질 우려는 완화될 것으로 판단
한다. 노사 협상은 여전히 진행 중이나 생산 안정성에 대한 고객사 우려가 낮아질 경
우 신규 수주에도 긍정적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 22

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 2,000,000원

TUẦN 26-30/10 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -32.53 điểm tương ứng -1.83%, với thanh 
khoản suy giảm

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
Mặc dù rủi ro mở rộng thêm nhịp điều chỉnh vẫn còn để ngỏ, tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn đang được bảo lưu, VNINDEX được kỳ vọng sẽ cho phản 
ứng hồi phục trở lại tại các điểm đỡ bên dưới

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -32.53 điểm tương ứng -1.83%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Linh kiện ô tô, Dầu khí và Công nghệ là các nhóm ngành có m ức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -634 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VPL; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-609 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -5,829 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -1,462 hợp đồng. Tự doanh duy trì mua ròng 722 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -5,278 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam đánh mất đà hồi phục của tuần trước, khi VN-Index quay đầu giảm khoảng 
1,8% về đóng cửa quanh 1.742 điểm, với phiên cuối tuần có thời điểm lùi sâu về sát 1.727 
điểm trước khi thu hẹp đà giảm. Dù mùa báo cáo KQKD quý 3 diễn ra tương đối tích cực, 
tuy nhiên các lo ngại về việc môi trường lãi suất cao vẫn duy trì trong bối cảnh căng thẳng 
Trung Đông leo thang đang đè nặng lên tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Kết quả kỳ họp của FED trong 
tuần qua nhìn chung phù hợp với kỳ vọng và không gây ra ảnh hưởng đáng kể. Điểm kém 
tích cực nhất là khối ngoại đã quay lại bán ròng với tổng giá trị khoảng 2.700 tỷ đồng 
trong tuần — đảo ngược diễn biến mua ròng cuối tuần trước, cho thấy đà rút vốn ngoại 
vẫn chưa thực sự dừng lại.

Chỉ số VNINDEX

Source: Trading View, KB Securities Vietnam

Ngắn hạn 
(Dưới 3 tháng)

Trung hạn (Từ 3 đến 9 tháng)

Chỉ báo  Nhận định xu hướng Hành động 
Xanh đậm  Khả năng tăng điểm cao Mua mạnh 
Xanh nhạt  Khả năng tăng điểm vừa phải Mua từng phần 
Vàng  Xu hướng trung tính Mua/Bán trading 
Cam  Khả năng giảm điểm vừa phải Bán từng phần 
Đỏ  Khả năng giảm điểm cao Bán mạnh

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tuần qua hình thành mẫu nến giảm trên đồ thị tuần, nhưng chưa thoái lui quá 1 
nửa cây nến tăng của tuần trước, cùng thanh khoản được tiết giảm, hàm ý nhịp điều chỉnh 
vẫn trong phạm vi cho phép. Trên khung ngày, sau bốn phiên giằng co đầu tuần với thanh 
khoản thấp, chỉ số đã chịu áp lực sụt giảm mạnh hơn trong phiên cuối tuần, chớm xuống 
dưới đường tín hiệu chính giữa dải Bollinger, nhưng cho tín hiệu rút chân ngay sau đó, thể 
hiện áp lực bán tháo đã tạm thời được kiểm soát. Mặc dù rủi ro mở rộng thêm nhịp điều 
chỉnh vẫn còn để ngỏ, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn đang được bảo lưu, VNINDEX 
được kỳ vọng sẽ cho phản ứng hồi phục trở lại tại các điểm đỡ bên dưới.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1850 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1800 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1720 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1680 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

trưởng 6%-8%/năm

Document 308: Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 12
Status: ok

### Page 1

By Advanced Economies Team
Advanced Economies:
Resilience to shock after shock
October 2026

### Page 2

• The global economy is in the midst of a growth cycle. As a result, GDP growth is more resilient to shocks than initially
anticipated.
• The figures for the second quarter bear this out, despite the sharp rise in oil prices, whose negative impact on growth is offset
by a series of reflationary forces.
• The driving force behind the current growth cycle is clear: artificial intelligence (AI).
• The world’s major economies are currently engaged in a broader push to expand their capital goods investment.

• While European and US households are experiencing more or less the same energy price shock, inflation and household
consumption remain higher in the US.
• Higher and above-target inflation calls for monetary tightening.
• Inflation is exceeding the target set by three of the four central banks examined in this publication (the US, the UK, the
Eurozone). Inflation is currently expected to peak in Q4 2026 or Q1 2027, but there is a significant risk that energy inflation will

spread further to other components of inflation in the future.
• Inflation can also be a sign of (too much) economic strength (in the US).
• Monetary policies also have to offset the inflationary effects of fiscal policy (particularly in Japan).
• The outbreak of the conflict in Iran acted as a catalyst: the ensuing rise in energy prices contributes to the recent increase in
long-term rates (which had been trending downwards at the start of the year) to levels close to those of 2002.

• The other issue is the high level of fiscal deficits (too high for a period of growth), reflecting different fiscal trajectories. In the
US, the UK, France and Germany the public debt-to-GDP ratio is increasing, while it has stabilized in Italy and decreased in
Spain and Japan.
• The rise in long-term interest rates will weigh on public finances. The fiscal arithmetic is simpler today than it will be in 2030.
By 2030, the effective rate will have risen and caught up with GDP growth.

For more information, please refer to our publication:
EcoPerspectives – Advanced Economies | 3rd quarter of 2026, as of September 24, 2026– Economic Research– BNP Paribas
Advanced economies: Resilience to shock after shock

Document 324: Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation

Source PDF: all report pdf/Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

Downgrade of global 
equities to a Core allocation  
Summary 
Event: We are downgrading global equities from Overweight 
to a Neutral/Core allocation. We continue to maintain a 
Neutral allocation to fixed income, Overweight on Gold and 
Underweight on Cash. There are more details in our Global 
Market Outlook publication on 30 October 2026. 
What does the equity downgrade mean?  
This downgrade implies a slightly lower allocation to global 
equities within a diversified portfolio. For our CIO Foundation 
Balanced portfolio, this would imply an equity allocation of 
52.5%, down from 53 .2%. We continue to expect positive 
returns for global equities  in the coming 6 -12 months , 
though equity investors may need to recalibrate expectations 
lower, compared with the 15 -20% annualised gains 
experienced in the last few years. Our capital market 
assumptions still suggest a mid -to-high single digit annual 
returns over the next few years, consistent with a transition 
to a mid -cycle bull market . Consensus estimates point to 
16% earnings growth for global equities in 2027, which 
should overcome various headwinds to deliver positive 
returns for equities over the next 12 months. Investors should 
thus, continue to maintain exposure to equities  within a 
diversified portfolio. 
In addition, we see a changing playbook  to prepare for 
higher volatility, more active management and greater 
diversification, as discussed below. 
What does it mean for regional equities 
positioning? 
Within a global equities portfolio, we remain Overweight US 
and Asia ex -Japan (AxJ) equities , which are expected to 
deliver the highest earnings growth in 2027 amongst the 
major regions. This growth is led by the technology sector, 
which carries the largest weight in both regions. Within the 
diversified equities portfolio, we maintain Core allocations to

Allocation to global equities decrease to 52.5% from 
53.2% for our CIO Foundation Balanced portfolio 
Foundation tactical asset allocation

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 30 October 2026

Euro area equities, which offer lower valuations, and Japan 
equities, which is backed by a reflating economy. We are 
Underweight UK equities, where the sector composition 
offers lower growth prospects in 2027. Within the AxJ region, 
we are Overweight Taiwan equities, with attractive structural 
growth driven by its semiconductor industry. We are also 
Overweight India equities, which offers diversified non-tech 
growth drivers. Taiwan and India equities both generate 
attractive returns on equity. We maintain Core allocations to 
China equities, with low valuations, and Korea equities, with 
explosive earnings growth in its semiconductors industry, but 
volatile price movements. We are Underweight ASEAN, 
which offers relatively lower earnings growth. 
What are the implications for equity sectors? 
We are Overweight these sectors globally: US 
information technology, US healthcare, US financials, US 
materials, Europe ex -UK financials, and Japan 
financials. 
A framework we use is to break the market into four key 
sector building blocks. Tech is the largest block, 
encompassing the Information Technology, Communication 
Services and Consumer Discretionary sectors. Put 
differently, the segment spans five major industries: 
semiconductors, hardware, internet, software, and IT 
services. Within the US market, we expect this segment to 
deliver c.30% earnings growth in 2027, extending the strong 
momentum seen in 2026. 
Financials and Real Estate  are the next building block, 
comprising these two sectors. We expect this segment to 
continue delivering low -teens earnings growth in 2027, 
supported by resilient economic activity and healthy credit 
trends. 
Resources include companies across four sectors: 
Industrials, Materials, Energy, and Utilities . We expect the 
segment to generate 10 -15% earnings growth in 2027, 
although Energy is likely to remain the largest drag on overall 
growth due to tougher comparisons and softer energy price 
assumptions for 2027. 
Healthcare & Others  consists primarily of Healthcare and 
Consumer Staples. We expect mid-teen earnings growth for 
the segment in 2027, with a rebound in healthcare earnings 
representing a potential upside surprise. 
Our Overweight sector exposures are drawn from each of the 
four key blocks above. Unlike the last few years , with 
concentrated gains from Tech providing market leadership, 
we see our downgrade in global equities also reflecting a 
broadening in market leadership towards each of the other 
sector blocks apart from Tech.

As structural themes such as AI transition from the rapid 
buildout phase towards monetisation, diversified exposure 
across sectors and regions should become increasingly 
important. 
What are the implications for equity 
opportunistic ideas? 
We have opportunistic ideas on Global High -Dividend 
equities, US healthcare, Euro area banks , MSCI 
Switzerland and Japan banks. The selection represents a 
more defensive stance  currently, with an average beta of 
below 1 relative to global equities (MSCI AC World), but 
attractive drivers nonetheless, for each of the ideas.  
Global High-Dividend offers higher-income equity allocation 
with historically lower volatility than global equities. US 
healthcare is supported by wider adoption of obesity 
treatments, advance s in oncology a nd innovative medical 
devices, that support earnings growth. Euro area banks  
continue to see steady earnings growth adding to their 
excess capital, which is being returned to shareholders via 
attractive dividend and share buyback yields. Japan banks 
continue to enjoy net margin expansion from BoJ policy 
normalisation, as well as corporate governance 
improvements. MSCI Switzerland is a newly initiated idea for 
investors seeking defensive exposure. H ealthcare and 
consumer staples account s for more than half the index 
weight, providing exposure to relatively resilient demand that 
is more insulated from economic cycles and AI trends. It also 
has a sizeable financial sector supported by steady wealth 
management growth and well-capitalised insurers with 
disciplined underwriting and resilient premium income. 
We see the case for more active management  in a mid -
cycle transition, as higher market dispersion should create 
greater opportunities for active sector and stock selection, 
particularly as leadership within equities evolves. 
What are the implications of higher equity 
market volatility?  
A more uncertain political backdrop and p otentially maturing 
growth rates in certain market segments may contribute to 
more frequent bouts of market volatility. Higher volatility 
presents an opportunity to use structured strategies to gain 
exposure to equities. Such strategies may be used to 
generate income or to gain exposure at a discounted entry 
price. Investors can also consider strategies that are 
structured to return principal or partial principal at maturity. 
What alternative strategies are suited to a 
regime of moderating equity market returns?  
Investors can consider alternative strategies such as hedge  
fund strategies or private markets, for those with the capacity

to accept some illiquidity.  
Equity long/short hedge fund  strategies would retain growth 
participation with less market exposure. Fund managers buy 
companies they expect to outperform and short those they 
expect to underperform, seeking to earn the difference 
between the two , and benefit even as total equity market 
returns are moderating. 
Within private markets , private equity provides  a means to 
higher returns with the effects of an illiquidity premium. 
Meanwhile, i nfrastructure offers  differentiated exposure to 
structural growth opportunities, where c ontracted long-term 
revenues reduce dependence on equity-market sentiment. 
What are the implications on fixed income 
and FX markets? 
Bonds: This year’s surge in bond yields means bonds now 
offer a hedge against any equity market weakness. We 
believe the 3 -7-year part of the US bond yield curve offers 
the best hedge against any equity sell -off caused by AI -
related growth worries. This part of the bond curve has priced 
in most Fed rate hike expectations, which could reverse if 
growth concerns return. Meanwhile, we favour US Treasury 
Inflation-Protected Securities (TIPS) as a hedge against any 
fuel price-led inflation scare.  
Credit: For corporate bonds, adopting a defensive stance in 
a volatile environment typically involves: i) increasing credit 
quality, favouring higher-rated issuers; and ii) moving up the 
capital structure, by reducing exposure to subordinated debt 
and favouring secured debt. From a sector perspective, 
large, diversified banks, utilities, and quasi -sovereigns tend 
to be more defensive, particularly relative to cyclical sectors, 
such as metals and mining, exploration, and technology.         
FX: We favour bearish AUD/JPY as a tactical hedge against 
any equity market correction. Review of 21 past S&P500 
correction sessions (2010 -2025) shows AUD weakened 
against USD in 86% of cases, while JPY strengthened 
against USD in 67%, supporting our bearish AUD/JPY 
outlook during any equity market stress.

Document 366: LatAm’s silicon dividend_ tailwinds from the AI investment boom in the U.S. (2026-10-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/LatAm’s silicon dividend_ tailwinds from the AI investment boom in the U.S. (2026-10-28).pdf
Report source: DB Research
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

Deutsche Bank 
Research Institute

LatAm’s silicon dividend: 
tailwinds from the AI  
investment boom in the U.S.

1 
Authors 
Fancisco Campos 
Chief LatAm Economist

Beatriz Garcia Nunes 
LatAm Economist

October 28, 2026 
Key messages: 
- U.S. investment has broken away from Europe and China since 2023, 
powered by AI-related equipment, software and research spending. 
- The AI CapEx boom demands commodities and merchandise that LatAm 
can supply.  
- Latin America’s export gains are real but concentrated. Copper, computer 
and electronics products provide the clearest links to the U.S. AI buildout. 
- Chile is the region’s most direct copper beneficiary. Mexico captures the 
windfall stemming from increases hardware demand and trade-diversion. In 
these cases, the AI-related tailwinds have undergirded appreciable 
improvements in external accounts. 
- The cases for nickel and zinc illustrate that the dividend is not widespread. 
U.S. imports of the former have mostly returned toward pre-pandemic levels, 
while for the latter bilateral gains partly reflect supplier substitution. 
- While expanding AI use, supply shortages and incentives can extend the 
CapEx boom, its financial viability poses downside risks. 
Another ‘American exceptionalism’ story  
Investment has become one of the sharpest dividing lines among the world’s 
core economic blocs. Since 2024, U.S. gross fixed capital formation has firmed 
up appreciably. Meanwhile, investment in Europe has broadly stalled while in 
China’s it has retreated, the drivers of which are beyond the scope of this report 
(Figure 1).  
The U.S. divergence has largely been an AI story: a burst of spending on 
computing power, digital infrastructure and intellectual property has offset 
weakness in conventional construction and housing. Indeed, total U.S. fixed 
investment is nearly 12% above its 2023 level. Within it, investment in 
information-processing equipment has risen by roughly 57%, software outlays by 
about 35%, and research and development by around 15%. By contrast, non-
residential structures and residential investment sit below their 2023 levels 
(Figure 2).

### Page 2

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 2

Figure 1: The global investment divergence

Figure 2: AI-related categories lead U.S. investment

Source: US Bureau of Economic Analysis, Eurostat, National Bureau of Statistics of 
China, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Source: US Bureau of Economic Analysis, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Latin America’s commodity channel 
AI is not merely a semiconductor story. Chips require specialized minerals; data 
centers require electricity and cooling; electricity requires grids, transformers and 
storage. That chain pulls in copper, aluminum, silver, tin, lithium and selected 
semiconductor inputs, along with natural gas and uranium. Latin America has 
meaningful exposure to several of these inputs, but copper has emerged as the 
commodity with the cleanest and most economically significant regional link. 
Copper is used throughout the AI infrastructure stack, including power cables, 
substations, transformers, server boards, heat sinks and cooling systems. Rising 
U.S. copper demand has lifted imports from Chile and Peru, particularly the 
former (Figure 3). T he effect matters beyond merchandise export volumes. 
Favorable terms-of-trade have helped Chile generate a current -account surplus 
for the first time in roughly fifteen years (0.2% of GDP as of Q2 2026 on a 4 -
quarter rolling basis ) and have allowed Peru to log the largest surplus in about 
forty-five years (4.5% of GDP as of Q2 2026 on a 4-quarter rolling basis).

28 October 2026

### Page 3

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 3

Figure 3: Copper epitomized the commodity link

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Not every metal is copper 
The broader mining story demands more caution, though. U.S. nickel imports have 
recovered toward pre-Covid levels, but the upswing has not displayed the same 
persistence as copper. Mexico has captured a disproportionate share of the 
bilateral improvement, yet that gain looks more like normalization than a 
sustained AI -driven break (Figure 4). Zinc tells a similar story. U.S. real imports 
stand shy of their pre-pandemic levels, and stronger purchases from Mexico and 
Peru partly reflect a reallocation away from Brazil rather than a decisive increase 
in U.S. demand (Figure 5).

Figure 4: Nickel: recovery, not a boom

Figure 5: Zinc: a substitution story

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research

28 October 2026

### Page 4

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 4

Mexico’s hardware advantage 
The larger non-commodity prize is in computers and electronics. U.S. imports of 
these products have sky -rocketed, while the supplier mix has shifted away from 
China. Mexico and various Asian economies have gained share on the back of this 
double-whammy impulse (Figure 6). 
This helps explain why Mexico’s non-automotive manufacturing exports, notably 
electronics, have outpaced automotive shipments to the U.S. and pressures from 
higher oil prices. The resulting non -oil trade surplus has offset pressure from the 
oil balance and helped Mexico generate a balanced current account (0% of GDP 
as of Q2 2026 on a 4 -quarter rolling basis). The external boost is meaningful, 
although it sho uld not necessarily be interpreted as bona fide  evidence of 
nearshoring, in our view.

Figure 6: U.S. computer & electronics imports going into overdrive

Source: U.S. Census Bureau, Haver Analytics and Deutsche Bank Research.

How much runway remains? 
Quantifying or mapping out a timeframe for the AI investment outlook is beyond 
the scope of this piece or our expertise. Nevertheless, we’d venture to argue that 
the capex cycle appears more likely to mature gradually than to stop abruptly . 
One reason is that the investment case is shifting from frontier -model training 
toward enterprise inference . Capacity that once served development can 
increasingly run commercial workloads for companies. That creates a path to 
revenue even if artificial general intelligence (AGI) crystallizes later than 
expected. It also spreads demand to a much larger corporate customer base.  
A second reason is scarcity. Graphics processing units (GPUs) remain tight, while 
power availability, grid connections, cooling equipment, construction capacity 
and data -center complexity limit how quickly supply can respond. Bottlenecks 
may therefore exte nd the cycle. The constraint is especially relevant for copper 
28 October 2026

### Page 5

LatAm Thematic Research

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 5

and power infrastructure, where projects have long lead times and permitting 
hurdles. 
A third reason is strategic. AI development resembles a prisoner’s dilemma at 
both the corporate and national levels. Each participant may worry about costs, 
safety and control, yet slowing unilaterally risks surrendering technological, 
commercial or natio nal security advantages. The same tension is visible in calls 
for guardrails alongside continued spending. Competition can therefore sustain 
capex even when short-term returns are uncertain. 
We acknowledge the risks, nonetheless. Higher borrowing costs raise the hurdle 
rate for an infrastructure buildout financed partly through debt. More important, 
revenues must eventually justify the installed capital base. The bearish case is not 
that AI la cks value; it is that infrastructure spending may run ahead of 
monetization, compromising some companies’ repayment capacity. Public 
opposition to the development of AI infrastructure and/or adoption of AI 
technologies could also slow down the technology’s roll-out. 
The regional balance  
Summing up, the AI investment boom in the U .S. is delivering a dividend to parts 
of Latin America, not the region as a whole. Chile and Peru benefit through copper 
prices and export volumes. Mexico benefits through electronics manufacturing 
and the replacement of Chinese supply. For Brazil and Colombia, the AI dividend 
is rather muted. Where materialized, furthermore, the dividend supports trade 
and current accounts more clearly rather than improving the domestic 
investment. 
These dynamics should persist while U.S. AI investment remains supply -
constrained and strategically protected. If widespread adoption advances and 
the strategic race intensifies, the impulse can last. If financing costs rise further 
or monetization disappo ints, the cycle may narrow. Either way, copper and 
Mexican electronics remain the region’s most visible gauges of the U.S. AI 
investment appetite.

Global backdrop

The global landscape remains complex, 
shaped by the interplay of AI, energy 
prices and tariffs, with significant 
implications for growth, inflation and 
financial conditions.

1) AI

The debate over the durability of the AI 
investment boom continues.  Tailwinds 
include technological advances, broader 
adoption, and s trategic and geopolitical 
considerations. Headwinds range from 
uncertainty over investment returns  and 
hyperscaler financing pressure to 
physical bottlenecks , safety concerns, 
and regulatory uncertainty.

Hyperscaler financing pressure has 
attracted increasing attention . In the 
early stages of the AI investment boom, 
hyperscaler capex was largely financed 
by internal cash flow. This is now 
changing, as free cash flow is expected 
to turn negative from 4Q, requiring major 
players to rely on debt issuance and 
other external financing increasingly.

At the same time, higher global interest 
rates are raising the cost of AI 
investment. The Fed resumed rate hikes 
at its September meeting, while global 
bond yields remain under upward 
pressure amid persistent inflation and 
elevated public and private borro wing 
needs. Our baseline assumes US rates 
will rise by 50bps this year and a further 
25bps in 2027 to 4.50%, with global 
long-term rates also remaining under 
pressure. This could increasingly weigh 
on the economics and pace of AI 
investment.

US-China AI competition is also drawing 
increasing attention.  Across the five 
layers of the AI ecosystem, China is 
making rapid progress in energy, 
infrastructure, AI models and 
applications, supported by its large scale, 
cost-effective domestic manufacturing 
supply chains and huge consumer 
market, which provides a wide range of 
real-world scenarios for AI adoption. The 
emergence of a more integrated and 
cost-competitive Chinese AI ecosystem 
could increasingly challe nge the US 
ecosystem, which currently operates at a 
relatively higher cost.

China, however, remains relatively weak 
in semiconductors.  Around half of 
China's domestic AI chip market still 
relies on foreign suppliers, including 
lower-end NVIDIA chips. This 
semiconductor bottleneck remains a key 
constraint on China's AI development. In 
our view, Taiwanese semiconductor 
companies' competiti ve moat therefore 
remains largely intact in the near term.

to 39% in 2027. Similarly, global 
semiconductor sales remained strong 
through 3Q, while WSTS expects growth 
to moderate from 90% this year to 27 % 
in 2027.

2) Energy

The US -Iran conflict remains at a 
standoff, keeping the energy -price 
outlook highly uncertain.  New 
developments are likely after the US 
midterm elections in November, with 
both further escalation and de -
escalation possible. Crude oil prices have 
remained elevated at around 
USD100/bbl amid a persistent 
geopolitical risk premium . In contrast, 
refined product prices have risen more 
sharply as refining disruptions and 
tighter product logistics have amplified 
the supply shock. Natural gas prices 
have also increased sharply, reflecting 
disruptions to LNG supply and limited 
alternative shipping routes.

Taiwan has been actively diversifying 
its energy import sources to cushion the 
impact. LNG imports from Qatar have 
fallen to zero since April, while imports 
from the US and other suppliers have 
surged to offset the shortfall, helping 
maintain stable domestic electricity 
supply.

3) Tariffs

US tariff actions are likely to accelerate 
after the US midterm elections in 
November. Two major tariff measures 
remain in the pipeline. The Section 301 
forced-labor tariff was implemented in 
July, while final tariff action under the 
Section 301 overcapacity investigation, 
which covers 16 economies, remains 
pending. Meanwhile, Phase 1 of the 
Section 232 semiconductor tariffs was 
implemented in January, while Phase 2, 
covering a broader range of 
semiconductor-related products, 
remains pending.

Taiwan has secured a relatively 
favourable framework through its trade 
and investment agreements with the 
US. Taiwan signed an Agreement on 
Reciprocal Trade (ART) and an 
Investment MOU with the US earlier this 
year. The ART establishes a 15% US tariff 
rate on Taiwanese goods, while the MOU 
provides Taiwan with preferential 
treatment under Section 232, including 
for semiconductors. The ART has yet to 
enter into force, and it remains to be 
seen how the final Section 301 tariff 
burden will interact with the 15% ART 
framework. Meanwhile, major Taiwanese 
companies such as TSMC are expanding 
their US investments under the 
Investment MOU, which could support 
relatively favourable treatment for 
Taiwan when broader Section 232 
semiconductor tariffs are introduced.

Taiwan 4Q outlook

Taiwan has navigated the complex 
global environment relatively well so far 
this year, supported by robust AI exports 
and investment, diversified energy 
imports, and efforts to absorb higher 
upstream energy costs. Some 
normalisation is likely in 4Q26 –2027, as 
the AI -driven growth boost moderates 
and domestic energy prices face greater 
pressure to adjust.

1) Growth

GDP growth remains broadly on track 
with our expectations. High -frequency 
indicators suggest that GDP growth 
should remain around 12% yoy in 3Q, 
broadly in line with 12.9% in 2Q but below 
the 15.4% peak in 1Q. Growth is expected 
to moderate to a high -single-digit pace 
from 4Q onwards. We maintain our full -
year GDP growth forecasts at 11.6% for 
2026 and 5.6% for 2027.

The AI cycle remains the key driver of 
Taiwan's growth.  Information and 
communication products and electronic 
components accounted for nearly 80% 
of total exports in the first nine months 
of this year, while manufacturing 
accounts for nearly 50% of gross fixed

capital formation. From 4Q onwards, 
export growth is expected to moderate 
from exceptionally strong levels to a 
more normal but still robust pace, 
broadly following the trajectory of 
global hyperscaler capex and 
semiconductor sales. Investment is likely 
to follow a similar path.

For now, Taiwanese technology 
companies continue to report robust 
revenue growth, led by semiconductors, 
ODMs/EMS, and PCs and servers. 
Capacity utilisation at leading Taiwanese 
foundries has also continued to rise, 
suggesting that investment demand 
remains strong.

The AI boom is also beginning to 
generate broader spillovers to the 
domestic economy. The AI-driven equity 
rally has generated significant wealth 
effects, with the TAIEX up 70% year-to-
date and Taiwan becoming the world's 
fifth-largest stock market. Taiwanese 
households have benefited from the 
appreciation of financial assets, with 
equities and mutual funds accounting for 
nearly 30% of total household financial 
assets. The equity rally has also 
generated positive income effects. Wage 
growth has picked up, led by the services 
sector, particularly financial services, as 
strong earnings at financial 
institutions—especially securities 
firms—have prompted higher 
compensation to retain talent. These 
wealth and income gains should support 
household consumption, creating a 
positive feedback loop that broadens the 
benefits of the AI boom across the wider 
economy.

2) Inflation

CPI inflation is also tracking broadly in 
line with our forecast. Headline CPI 
remained elevated at 2.4% yoy in 3Q, up 
from 1.2% in 1Q and 2.2% in 2Q. We 
maintain our forecast for headline CPI 
inflation to remain close to 2.5% in 4Q, 
and retain our full-year forecasts at 2.0% 
for 2026 and 2.1% for 2027.

Energy inflation remains the main 
upside risk.  The government has 
absorbed much of the increase in energy 
costs through state -owned CPC and 
Taipower, limiting the pass -through to 
domestic consumers. With losses at both 
companies widening, the government 
will need to decide whether to provide 
additional subsidies to cover the losses 
or allow further increases in domestic 
energy prices. The September re view 
postponed the adjustment to the 
December meeting, when the overall 
situation will be reassessed after the 
November local elections. Taipower's  
latest review indicated that electricity 
rates would need to rise by 12.8%. A hike 
in electricity prices could lift inflation 
expectations and generate second -
round effects, particularly as the labour 
market tightens and wage pressures 
increase.

3) Monetary policy

The central bank kept the policy 
discount rate unchanged at 2.00% at its 
September meeting, in line with our 
expectations. We continue to expect a 
modest 12.5bp rate hike at the 
December meeting, bringing the policy 
rate to 2.125%.  By then, CPI inflation is 
likely to have remained above 2% for 
around six consecutive months. The 
possibility of an increase in electricity 
prices following the November local 
elections could further lift inflation 
expectations, strengthening the case for 
a December rate hike.

Financial conditions have already 
tightened despite the absence of a 
policy rate hike.  M2 growth remained 
around 7% yoy in 3Q, well below double-
digit nominal GDP growth. At the same 
time, bank loan growth has accelerated 
to more than 10% yoy, driven by strong

corporate credit demand, particularly AI-
related fundraising by technology 
companies. Tighter financial conditions 
are also evident in rising market interest 
rates, including commercial paper rates 
and government bond yields.

The central bank has continued to ease 
housing credit controls gradually. At its 
September meeting, it raised the LTV 
cap on second -home mortgage loans to 
70%, the second easing of housing credit 
controls this year. There is scope for 
further easing, including removing the 
LTV cap on second-home loans or raising 
the LTV caps for third - and fourth-home 
loans and high -value properties, which 
currently stand at 30%. However, the 
central bank is likely to proceed 
cautiously, given concerns that a faster 
relaxation could reignite  speculative 
demand and put renewed upward 
pressure on housing prices.

4) Housing

A gradual and modest recovery in the 
housing market is expected from 4Q26 
into 2027.  The existing -home market 
has already shown early signs of 
recovery, with Sinyi  housing prices 
returning to slightly positive yoy growth 
since 2Q. Total building transactions also 
returned to positive yoy growth in 3Q. 
The easing of credit controls should help 
reinforce this modest recovery trend.

The new -home market remains under 
pressure. The number of new units under 
construction has started to decline since 
2023, while new completions are  only 
beginning to ease this year . Demand 
remains weak, as potential buyers 
continue to wait for lower prices. Unsold 
new homes stood at 113K units at end -
2025, compared with a historical normal 
level of around 80K, implying excess 
inventory of roughly 30K units. At the 
current pace of new -home transactions, 
it would take roughly one year to 
normalize the existing excess inventory, 
and longer when factoring in the new 
supply pipeline. Some industry experts 
estimate that it could take as long as 3.8 
years digest current inventories fully 
(here).

要支撐，AI類股修正時期資金仍在其他類股間
輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢
熱門議題/資產影響推估 市場焦點
➢ 美國國會變天： 民主黨不僅重新奪回眾議院 ，也
以遠超預期的幅度拿下參議院多數席次 。整體政
策方向其實不會發生重大改變 ，但民主黨未來可
能利用財政法案與債務上限談判 ，推動限制對伊
朗戰爭、部分撤銷關稅以及影響財政支出。
➢ 市場焦點放在資料中心建置進程 ：AI資料中心的
建設是否受到影響 ，是目前市場最關注的焦點 。
本行認為短期逆風確實存在 ，但不影響長期建置

美國國會變天，AI概念受挫然未見風險情緒，殖利率回落
AI概念受挫，資金輪動至其他板塊
盤勢回顧
美10年期利率回到5%以下，信用利差自高點收斂
3
• AI概念受挫，資金輪動至其他板塊：民主黨全面掌控國會後，市場擔憂AI產業、大型雲端服務與資料中心的監
管與審查將顯著收緊，使相關類股、日台韓股市均下挫，不過資金輪動到其他產業，支撐美股指數維持穩定。
• 財政紛擾仍存但支出展望中性 ，美10年債利率來到5%以下：市場開始反應財政政策可望趨於中性 ，再加上地
緣政治有望緩和，布蘭特原油跌破100美元/桶，支持美國公債利率持續走低至5%以下，自10月高點以來下滑
近50個基點。殖利率下滑輔以金融市場不確定因素降低支撐股市反彈，同步帶動美國信用債利差收斂，11月以
來美國信用債總報酬緩步走揚。
資料來源：左， Bloomberg ，2026/11/5，右，Bloomberg，2026/11/5。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11/5

©  CTBC
兩黨對立將放大財政期限影響與紛擾，整體財政支出趨於中性
兩黨對立，財政期限的重要性上升
政策影響
2027年的財政動能預計將大致維持中性
4
• 財政期限的重要性上升，帶來政治紛擾：民主黨可利用具強制性的時間節點，作為政策讓步的槓桿。第一個要
注意的時間點是12/11政府支出授權到期，不過將期限延到2027年2至3月的機率較高(否則政府可能關門)。另
外推論1Q27將臨近債務上限，以目前的財政部存款和非常規措施的使用，最多可支應至2027年8月至10月。
• 財政政策整體趨於中性：分裂政府通常意味著較不具擴張性的財政政策，而眾議院掌握聯邦預算的撥款權，將
可能透過拒絕追加或直接限制戰爭預算的方式來迫使戰事降溫 。然民主黨同時也可能與共和黨協議，以撤銷去
年大而美法案的支出削減措施、恢復健康保險補貼等作為交換條件，讓白宮得以增加國防預算。綜合來看，短
線面臨預算協調的紛擾，美債殖利率波動仍可能偏高，但趨勢上政策制衡效果增強，有利減輕殖利率的壓力。
資料來源：左， 高盛，2026/11，右，高盛，2026/11。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11

©  CTBC
關稅結構可能改變，資料中心監管程度提高但不影響建置趨勢
民主黨將嘗試撤回部分關稅，但整體稅率變化有限
政策影響
對資料中心監管程度將提高，但非禁止建置
5
• 民主黨將嘗試撤回部分關稅 ，但整體稅率變化可能有限 ：民主黨領袖已表示將嘗試撤銷已實施的部分關稅，但
川普政府仍可利用301條款推動新的關稅，整體有效關稅稅率變化可能有限，但其中可能的重點是降低對加拿大
的關稅。
• 對資料中心監管程度將提高，不影響建置趨勢：傳統共和黨票倉的德州變天，而德州是最大的資料中心建設地
區，新任的民主黨州長可能要求企業自行承擔電力及電網擴建成本 、保障居民用水優先權，並在保護規範明確
之前影響資料中心的建設進度。不過本行認為，除了要求企業揭露資料來源與訓練資訊、建立安全治理架構，
以及落實其他風險管理措施等規定之外，尚無太多州級監管制度被認為具有高度限制性。而在聯邦層級，大規
模的新監管措施即使被國會提出，也很可能被川普否決，且AI競爭力仍是跨黨派國策，建設趨勢將持續推進。
資料來源：左， 高盛 ，2026/10，右，高盛，2026/10。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10

### Page 6

©  CTBC
AI 資料中心已從「產業政策」轉變為「民生電價議題」
超大型業者算力供給轉吃緊
產業影響
2030年電力缺口收斂
6
• 德州資料中心暫停令，AI算力布局的轉變與供需影響：德州8月初頒布的暫停令，同步凍結了新資料中心的興建
（僅限未動工新案）與電力輸送。在扣除被淘汰的虛報專案需求後，預估部分專案將外溢至其他電力更吃緊的州，
導致全美電力缺口淨擴大約 3–6 GW。若Hinojosa 就任後延續暫停令執行超過一年，其影響2028 年全美電力缺
口將進一步擴大，2030年供需缺口預估將逐步收斂。另一方面，暫停令讓2028年本就吃緊的算力市場更緊，維
持算力租金的高檔區間，對超大型業者而言建置成本增加，與手上既有的算力可以提高售價，兩者相抵，2028
年企業獲利的預估影響僅在1-3%水準。
• 提高監管是正在發生的實質逆風，喊停是低機率的極端政治口號：AI 技術攸關美國的核心基本國力。民主黨的
政策向來主張「調整與監管」，而非全面「廢止」。因此，未來更嚴格的許可審查與補助限制，勢必會提高雲

端大廠在德州擴張的時間成本與資本風險。然而，也可能推動針對資料中心的特定稅種，例如聯邦總收入稅，
或針對資料中心電力消耗的消費稅。民主黨也有可能在新會期提出比共和黨更強制性更嚴格監管的版本 。民主
黨掌控國會後，最可能成法的是「電費成本分攤」立法，因為川普已將此議題列為自己的政績。
資料來源：左，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11，右，blackrock，2026/4。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11/4

©  CTBC
健護最具兩黨協商的價值，醫療產業相對受惠
12/11為醫療補貼可以放進談判的時間點
產業影響
健護未來一年的本益比仍合理
7
• ACA補貼及醫療補助是最可能與川普達成交易的領域 ：民主黨贏得參眾兩院，不等於民主黨執政，兩黨仍須協
商。就健護產業而言，相對有利影響的領域在2025年底已到期的<平價醫療法案(ACA)>增強型保費補貼，受淨保
費上升，已有超過200萬人放棄投保 ，及受<大而美法案 >被削減的醫療補助 (Medicaid)在2025~2034年超過
9,000億美元。現ACA進行增強型補貼的恢復提案還在進行中，需總統簽署才能立法恢復，可以是談判交換條件
的一部分；Medicaid刪減是已成法、分階段生效，無法被推翻，只能延緩Medicaid的刪減，其中對工作的要求
限制於今年底生效，早於新國會就任，12/11臨時撥款法案談判是可以納入談判作為局部修正空間的時間點。
• 健護是民主黨國會最直接的受惠產業，健護未來一年本益比仍相對合理：預期若醫療補助刪減延後，醫院將受

惠，ACA補貼若部分恢復，將有利ACA型管理式醫療業者的投保人數止跌回升。以健護未來一年本益比來看，
目前股價仍相對合理。
資料來源：左，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11，右，LSEG，2026/11/4。 中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11/5

資金輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢
期中選舉前股市較為承壓，但無礙趨勢
市場觀點
資料來源：(左) 高盛，2026/11；(右) 摩根大通，2026/11 。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11
分裂國會在兩年任期內的表現優於單一政黨執政
8
1974年以來標普500指數期中選舉前後的走勢
• 期中選舉前股市較為承壓，但無礙趨勢：根據歷史經驗，期中選舉年通常是總統任期中回撤較深的一年，且選
前的股市波動會明顯放大，然在選後不確定性消退，市場表現往往有所改善，標普500指數在選後的3個月，歷
史中位數報酬率為+6%，而分裂政府(總統和國會分屬不同黨派)的歷史報酬率通常較佳。
• AI相關類股波動高，資金輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢：真正驅動市場的因素仍是利率、地緣、
AI，而不是單純從歷史規律推演，近期市場擔憂雲端業者的現金流和債務問題，因此若資料中心的建置時程受
到影響，將進一步影響相關業者的營收和償債時程，提高AI相關類股的波動；另外眾議院是否對川普提出彈劾 ，

也將造成政治上的波動。但就整體趨勢來說，既有政策不會因此出現重大轉折，穩健成長的企業獲利提供重要
支撐，AI類股修正時期資金仍在其他類股間輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢。
1950 年以來，不同政治格局下的標普500指數兩年價格報酬率

### Page 9

©  CTBC
短線待消化北亞出口疑慮，長線行情回歸基本面
情緒回穩後聚焦基本面與地緣動盪 短線消化資料中心建設監管趨嚴對北亞出口影響，
然長線美核心政策延續性依舊，估計影響有限
9
• 選後反彈高度取決景氣與政策推演：回顧過去美股期中選舉期間，新興市場選前壓抑，選後大多繳出正報酬佳
績，上漲比率超越五成。以參眾兩院控制權皆翻轉的1994年、2006年來看，1944年因美聯準會啟動快速升息，
加上墨西哥金融危機爆發，選後新興市場進入近一年空頭走勢。2006年即便也是分裂政府，全球宏觀環境受惠
貿易繁榮、商品超級周期推動經濟成長，新興市場選後12個月累積報酬亮眼。總體來說，選後不確定性下降，
新興市場可望迎反彈走勢，而即便美國政治版圖翻轉，選後新興市場走勢取決於景氣、政策推演與地緣干擾。
• 市場短線待消化資料中心建設進程對北亞出口影響：美期中選舉對新興市場影響較大面向，主要是關稅與貿易
政策、債務上限，以及對資料中心建設的態度。關稅主導權在白宮推估影響有限 ; 預算與債限談判或對利率造
成波動 ; 短線需消化選後對資料中心監管趨嚴影響北亞出口的失望情緒，然預計長線AI建置仍屬國家重心政策。

資料來源：左， Bloomberg ，2026/11，右， Bloomberg ，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11。

• 期中選舉共和黨大敗，美元走弱：週三市場確認共和黨於期中選舉失利後，美元兌主要貨幣全面回檔。本次期
中選舉由民主黨取得參、眾兩院多數。民主黨掌控參議院後，可阻止或延後川普的人事任命；贏得眾議院後，
則可對政府展開調查，甚至進一步推動彈劾程序。歷史經驗顯示，當民主黨於期中選舉後同時掌控參、眾兩院
時，美元指數平均下跌 4.6%，6次中有4次下跌。整體而言，選舉結果短期內可能因預算協商及債務上限僵局

升高政治風險。2027年美國將再度面臨債務上限問題，美伊地緣政治及貿易戰等變數亦將牽動實質利率走勢。
• 2027年債務上限恐成兩黨鬥爭焦點 ，不利美元：12月11日前，現任國會通過持續撥款決議的可能性偏高 ，但
美國聯邦政府收支預計將於2027年觸及債務上限。受債務上限約束的債務規模預估於2027年3月接近法定上限
500億美元以內 ，之後隨 4月稅收入帳而暫時下降；但若財政赤字增幅高於預期 ，X-Day恐提前到來 。展望
2027年，美國財政議題仍將面臨逆風。共和黨失去完全執政雖有助抑制財政擴張，但政治僵局恐加劇債務上限、
違約及信評遭調降風險，使美元波動加大。

• 非核心消費板塊獲利前景近
期蒙上陰霾。首先，郵輪業
雖受惠於旅遊需求韌性而上
調 2026 財年預測，但高利
率與高油價恐使後市持續承
壓；其次，運動鞋服產業受
到大中華區市場衰退與新興
小品牌崛起雙重打擊，面臨
營收與獲利雙雙下滑的困境
，短期內組織重整效益恐難
顯現。再者，在即將迎來的
財報季前夕，市場共識普遍
預期 3Q26 的增長將陷入停
滯。
《市場共識預期3Q26非核心消費板塊成長停滯》
美3Q26財報季將陸續揭曉 ，目前市場共識預期標
普500指數各產業板塊第三季的獲利成長存在較大
差異，其中資訊科技和能源板塊將推動指數整體獲
利成長，預期非核心消費板塊整體每股盈餘將不會
成長。另外，零星個股捎來截至 8月底的季度業績
也顯示好壞參半的結果。
《郵輪旅遊需求仍具韌性，燃油成本仍是潛在風險》
嘉年華郵輪 (CCL)3Q26財年(截至8月底)營收年增
3.5%，每股盈餘持平 ，淨收益率超預期 ，主要貢
獻來自船上及其它收入，然因燃料成本大增36%，
完全抵銷了營運效率與成本控制的效益 ，導致調整

後EBITDA持平。管理層上調2026年全年獲利展望 ，
且公司表示 2027年訂位量及訂位價格均創下歷史
新高，目前已售出約半數 2027年艙位 ，顯示旅遊
需求仍具韌性。但市場對其未來半年的獲利預期偏

保守，主要是9 月以來居高不下的利率與一路攀升
的油價，或將直接推高其利息負擔與燃油開銷。由

《運動鞋服Nike轉型成效慢，獲利展望不佳》
Nike1Q27財年(2026/6~2026/8)營收與每股盈餘
皆衰退。運動休閒系列、Jordan品牌和大中華區市
場下滑 ，成為主要逆風 。管理層預期 2027財年營
收將出現高個位數下滑，提出的每股盈餘財測也低
於市場預期 。即便Nike推出名為 Pace營運重整計
畫，但效益明顯無法在短期內看到 。其中一個原因
是Nike的市場主導地位已不如前，小型品牌如今已

具備規模化營運能力，當消費者行為已發生結構性
轉變時(品牌忠誠度降低與多品牌嘗試 )，如何收復
市佔率就成為一項艱巨且持久的挑戰。
《2026 美年底消費總額預期創新高 ，逾半增長靠
「通膨」，電商與生成式AI成雙引擎》
近期已有多家研調機構針對美國2026年年底消費
旺季出具預測報告。整體來看，各大機構預測的銷
售總額皆創下歷史新高 ，但其背後的共同隱憂是
「通膨」，直言超過一半的名目增長率是由物價上
漲驅動。這使得消費者普遍展現「價格敏感、主動
尋找折扣、切換品牌以省錢」的價值導向行為。電
商增速(網購)依然大幅碾壓實體零售。所有機構也
都點出生成式AI在2026年成為主流購物工具。
資料來源：Mastercard， 2026/9/23，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/13

機構預測2026年底消費旺季
各家機構重點分述如下
Mastercard ： 預 期 假日消費成長近半因物價上漲 ，
今年主因能源與AI建設成本增加，去年則受關稅影
響。鑑於柴油價格持續高企可能推高零售商的運輸
成本，並對年底的整體消費價格構成上行壓力，因
此值得密切關注。高收入家庭因財富成長、增加自
由支配支出，成為近年消費成長的重要引領者， 然
而，受益於勞動市場穩定、薪資成長與健康資產負
債表，各收入群體亦持續穩定貢獻，消費仍由廣大
家庭共同支撐。
Adobe：預計今年假期季節將推出極具競爭力的折

扣，雖然消費者歷來都熱衷於利用節日促銷活動購
買禮物或添加最新電子產品，但許多人現在更傾向
於利用折扣囤積生活必需品。另，今年假期季節美
國零售網站的人工智慧流 量 將年增130%。預計感
恩節的增幅最大(年增159%)，其次是黑色星期五(
年成長 95%)和網路星期一(年增88%)。
德勤(Deloitte) ： 消費者仍然重視為親朋好友營造
特別的節日氛圍，同時也在精打細算地消費。為了
更好地利用每一分錢，觀察到各個收入階層的消費
者都在追求性價比，例如在不同品牌和零售商之間
切換，以及利用促銷活動來控制支出。

Document 512: コモディティ・レポート（2026年7～9月）

Source PDF: all report pdf/コモディティ・レポート（2026年7～9月）.pdf
Report source: MURC

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 20 日

調査レポート

コモディティ・レポート（2026 年 7～9 月）

調査部 主任研究員 芥田 知至  
1. コモディティ市況全般：原油が上昇傾向、銅は最高値更新、金は上値重い 
ドル建て国際商品市況全般の動向を示す FTSE・コアコモディティ CRB 指数は、2023 年 5 月 31 日に 2022
年 1 月以来の安値をつけた後、2026 年 3～9 月には大幅に上昇して 9 月 10 日に 2008 年 7 月以来の高値を
つけた。その後、10 月にかけてやや下落した。コモディティ相場は、中東情勢や米金融政策の行方を巡って、引
き続き、不安定な推移が見こまれる。

2. エネルギー市況：足元はブレントが一時 110ドル、WTI が 100 ドル前後 
国際指標のブレント原油は、2026 年 4 月 30 日に 126.41 ドルと 2022 年 3 月以来の高値をつけた。足元は
110 ドル前後で不安定な動きとなっている。米国とイランは戦闘終結やホルムズ海峡再開に向けた動きは停滞し
たままであり、緊迫する中東情勢を受けて原油は高止まりが見こまれる。

3. ベースメタル市況：銅は 9 月上旬に 14,875 ドルの史上最高値 
銅相場は、2026 年 9 月 10 日に 14,875 ドルと史上最高値を記録し、足元は 14,000 ドル台半ばを中心に推
移している。銅は、原油高や景気悪化の影響が懸念される一方で、 AI・エネルギー転換・防衛の関連分野を中
心に需要が伸びるとの見方が根強く、高値圏での一進一退が見こまれる。

4. 貴金属市況：金は 8 月中旬に 4,700 ドル近くまで持ち直すも足元は 4,100 ドル前後 
金は、2026 年 1 月 29 日に 5,594.82 ドルと史上最高値を記録した後、6 月 30 日には 3,942.99 ドルまで下
落し、足元は 4,100 ドル前後を中心に推移している。米利上げが続くとの観測が有力な中、中東情勢も安定化に
はまだ遠く、原油高が意識されて、金は買われにくい状況が続きやすいとみられる。

1. コモディティ市況全般の概況：原油が上昇傾向、銅は最高値更新、金は上値重い 
ドル建て国際商品市況全般の動向を示す FTSE・コアコモディティ CRB 指数（終値ベース）は、2022 年 6 月
9 日に 2011 年 9 月以来の高値をつけた後、2023 年 5 月 31 日には 2022 年 1 月以来の安値をつけた。2026
年 3～9 月に大幅に上昇し、9 月 10 日に 2008 年 7 月以来の高値をつけた。10 月にかけてやや下落した。 
マクロ経済環境をみると、フーシ派とサウジアラビアによる攻撃の応酬が激しさを増すなど中東の混乱に広がり
がみられ、米国とイランも戦闘終結やホルムズ海峡再開へ向けた動きは停滞したままである。原油は緊迫する中
東情勢を受けて上昇傾向で推移した。金は、原油高などを受けた根強い米利上げ観測を背景に上値が重い状
況が続いた。銅は、原油高や景気悪化が懸念される一方で、 AI・エネルギー転換・防衛の関連分野を中心に需
要が伸びるとの見方が根強く、史上最高値圏で一進一退となった。中東情勢や米金融政策を巡って、コモディテ
ィ相場は不安定な推移が続くと見こまれる。
